Extensive experiments demonstrate that DICS achieves comparable accuracy while substantially reducing training time across synthetic and benchmark datasets, highlighting the benefit of integrating data-informed priors into split selection for scalable classification tree learning.

</details>


### [177] [Dynamic Structural Causal Modeling for Sleep](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20285)
*Ranveer Singh,Saurabh Mathur,Pranuthi Tenali,Arun Badi,Sriraam Natarajan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文利用PCMCI+算法从105份家庭睡眠呼吸暂停测试（HSAT）记录中学习睡眠呼吸障碍的动态因果图，揭示不同性别和年龄亚组之间存在系统性的因果结构差异。研究发现时间自依赖和呼吸暂停-去饱和关系在所有队列中持续存在，而其他因果关系则显著变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 睡眠呼吸障碍的因果动态机制复杂且在不同患者群体间存在差异，这阻碍了针对性干预措施的发展。理解这些因果结构的异质性对于开发个性化的治疗方案至关重要。

Method: 使用PCMCI+算法对来自105份HSAT记录的窗口化分数变量进行动态因果图学习；利用领域知识进行边缘黑名单约束；采用bootstrap聚合（bagging）来处理小规模亚组样本量的问题。

Result: 学习到的因果图显示，时间自我依赖关系和呼吸暂停-去饱和关系在所有性别和年龄亚组中均保持一致，而其他因果关系则在各队列之间存在显著差异。

Conclusion: 睡眠呼吸障碍的因果结构在不同患者群体间既存在共性也存在差异，这些发现可能有助于更精准地指导针对特定人群的干预策略制定。

Abstract: The causal dynamics of sleep-disordered breathing are complex and vary across patient populations, hindering the development of targeted interventions. We learn dynamic causal graphs of sleep-disordered breathing from Home Sleep Apnea Test (HSAT) recordings, revealing systematic differences in causal structure across sex and age subcohorts. We do so using the PCMCI+ algorithm on windowed fractional variables derived from 105 HSAT recordings, exploiting domain knowledge via edge blacklisting and employing bootstrap aggregation to address small subcohort sizes. The learned graphs show that temporal self-dependencies and the apnea-desaturation relationship persist across all cohorts, while other relationships vary substantially.

</details>


### [178] [Physical-Support Confidence Sets for Highly Coherent Dictionaries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20295)
*Guan-Ju Peng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出分辨率感知的物理支撑推断方法，联合考虑字典学习不确定性和部署信号表示不确定性，通过跨字典置信对应筛选合理字典和表示，投影到物理支撑空间。建立了最优物理分辨率的极小极大界，并提出自适应有限候选评估算法AEB，避免物理上过度精确的推断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 字典学习后的稀疏追踪即使在物理解释无法由标定数据证实时，仍可能产生精确的原子支撑，尤其在高相干字典中，不同但标定兼容的字典可能对相同支撑赋予不同物理含义。现有方法忽略了字典学习中的不确定性，导致物理推断可能虚假精确。

Method: 提出跨字典置信对应方法：保留与标定数据兼容的字典和与部署信号兼容的稀疏表示，将存活的解释投影到物理支撑空间。对于局部相干原子类别（分离尺度s），推导极小极大物理分辨率界。提出自适应有限候选程序AEB（active endpoint bracketing），仅评估仍可能影响物理报告的候选，安全地粗化或弃权。

Result: 由N个标定信号得到的极小极大物理分辨率满足 δ_opt(N,s) ≍ min{s, 1/(√N s²)}，相对分辨率受方向信息尺度Ns⁶控制。部署复制仅在方向变化无法通过调整活动系数吸收时改善物理定位。有限候选实验（含四区域合成应用）表明点值插件选择器物理上过度精确，而AEB避免了不支持的细化且候选评估更少。

Conclusion: 物理支撑推断必须同时考虑字典学习和表示中的双重不确定性，仅依赖点估计的稀疏追踪会给出物理上过度精确的结果。AEB提供了计算高效且统计可信的替代方案。

Abstract: Sparse pursuit after dictionary learning can yield a precise atom support even when its physical interpretation is not justified by the calibration data, especially for highly coherent dictionaries where alternative calibration-compatible dictionaries may assign different physical meanings to the same selected support. We develop resolution-aware physical-support inference that jointly accounts for uncertainty in the learned dictionary and in the representation of a deployment signal. Our cross-dictionary confidence correspondence retains calibration-compatible dictionaries and deployment-compatible sparse representations, then projects the surviving explanations onto physical-support space. For local coherent-atom classes with separation scale s, once the deployment data resolve the coherent-block explanation and its atom support, the minimax physical resolution from N calibration signals satisfies $δ_{\mathrm{opt}}(N,s)\asymp\min\{s,\frac{1}{\sqrt{N}s^2}\}$, with relative resolution governed by the orientation-information scale $Ns^6$. Deployment replication improves physical localization only when orientation changes cannot be absorbed by adjusting the active coefficients. For computation, we introduce active endpoint bracketing (AEB), an adaptive finite-bank procedure that evaluates only candidates that can still affect the physical report and otherwise safely coarsens or abstains. Finite-bank experiments, including a four-region synthetic application, show that a point-valued plug-in selector can be physically overprecise, whereas AEB avoids unsupported refinement with fewer candidate evaluations.

</details>


### [179] [Explainable Transformer Models for Clinical Prediction Tasks on Structured Electronic Health Records](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20315)
*Jun Ni Du,Lukas Adamek,Maxim Kryukov,Flavio Dormont,Ziv Bar-Joseph,Sven Jager,Brandon Rufino*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: BERT-LER是一个BERT风格的EHR预测模型，基于7500万患者的去标识化数据预训练，通过百分位分箱将实验室检验结果编码为离散token保留分级信息，并结合Integrated Gradients实现token级别的可解释归因。在EHRShot基准和哮喘严重度进展研究上，其预测性能与公开基准模型相当（在实验室相关任务上常超越），且归因结果与临床已知风险因素一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有面向结构化电子健康记录（EHR）的预测模型很少同时强调定量实验室信息和对输入医疗事件的可解释性，存在方法学空白。

Method: 提出BERT-LER：一种BERT风格的编码EHR时间线模型，在7500万患者的去标识化EHR数据上预训练和微调；将实验室检验结果通过百分位分箱编码为离散token以保留分级信息；配合Integrated Gradients进行基于输入EHR序列的token级归因。

Result: 在EHRShot基准套件和基于真实世界数据的哮喘严重度进展研究中，BERT-LER的预测性能与公开基准模型相当，且在实验室相关任务上往往超过基准；提供的归因与临床已知的风险因素一致。

Conclusion: 该架构和可解释性方法可推广到使用结构化EHR训练的多种治疗领域和预测任务，弥合了实验室值表示与可解释性统一框架的空白。

Abstract: Predictive models over structured electronic health records (EHRs) remain central to machine learning for healthcare, but few have jointly emphasized quantitative laboratory information and interpretability with respect to input medical events. We present BERT-LER, a BERT-style model for coded EHR timelines pretrained and fine-tuned from a de-identified EHR dataset of 75 million patients, that encodes laboratory test results as discrete tokens while retaining graded information through percentile-based binning, paired with Integrated Gradients for token-level attributions grounded in the input EHR sequence. We benchmark our approach on the public EHRShot benchmark suite and on an asthma severity progression study based on real-world data. This addresses a methodological gap in EHR foundation-style modeling by unifying laboratory value representation and explainability in a single framework, while assessing whether both predictive performance and explanations generalize beyond standard clinical prediction tasks. Across EHRShot and asthma tasks, BERT-LER achieves predictive performance that is competitive with, and on laboratory-related tasks often exceeds, publicly available benchmark models, and provides attributions that align with clinically known risk factors. Our architecture and explainability approach can be applied to many therapeutic areas and prediction tasks using language models trained on structured EHRs.

</details>


### [180] [A comparison between ceiling-mounted FMCW, IR-UWB and Wi-Fi radar for in-bedroom human activity monitoring and sleep interruption detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20322)
*Anton Lambrecht,Reda El Hail,Xianjun Jiao,Pieter Crombez,Dominique Schreurs,Peter Karsmakers,Adnan Shahid,Eli De Poorter*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文在相同部署条件下对比了天花板安装的FMCW雷达、IR-UWB和Wi-Fi传感三种射频技术，使用20名参与者在6种房间布局下的同步记录，通过相同的CNN在10类HAR和4类睡眠监测任务上评估。IR-UWB在跨受试者活动识别上最优（89.0%宏F1），FMCW在跨房间泛化上最佳（83.8%宏F1），所有技术在睡眠监测中均超过92%宏F1。研究发现识别性能与环境鲁棒性之间存在根本性权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究通常在不同硬件、数据集和评估方法下比较FMCW、IR-UWB和Wi-Fi传感，缺乏在相同部署条件下的公平对比；同时天花板安装雷达在医疗环境中的性能和成本优势尚未得到充分研究。

Method: 采用受控对比实验设计，使用20名参与者、6种房间布局下的同步多技术记录，将FMCW、IR-UWB和Wi-Fi传感置于相同部署条件（天花板安装），并统一使用相同的CNN架构评估细粒度10类HAR任务和粗粒度4类睡眠监测任务。

Result: IR-UWB在跨受试者活动识别中表现最佳（89.0%宏F1），FMCW在跨未见房间布局的泛化上最优（83.8%宏F1），所有技术在睡眠监测任务中均超过92%宏F1。

Conclusion: 识别性能与环境鲁棒性之间存在根本性权衡，该权衡可通过距离分辨率、天线分集、多普勒分辨率和空间信息保留的差异加以解释，为面向医疗的射频感知系统设计提供了实践指导。

Abstract: Despite their growing importance for contact-free radio frequency (RF) based healthcare monitoring, different radio technologies such as frequency-modulated continuous wave (FMCW) radar, impulse radio ultra-wideband (IR-UWB), and Wi-Fi sensing are rarely compared under identical deployment conditions, as existing studies typically differ in hardware, datasets, and evaluation methodologies. In addition, the performance of ceiling-mounted radars, despite their practical deployment and cost advantages in healthcare environments, remain underexplored. Therefore, this paper presents a controlled comparison and analysis of ceiling-mounted FMCW, IR-UWB, and Wi-Fi sensing using synchronized recordings from 20 participants across six room layouts. All technologies are evaluated with the same convolutional neural network (CNN) on both a fine-grained 10-class human activity recognition (HAR) task and a coarse 4-class sleep monitoring task. IR-UWB achieves the highest cross-subject activity recognition performance (89.0% macro F1), while FMCW generalizes best to unseen room layouts (83.8% macro F1). For sleep monitoring, all technologies exceed 92% macro F1 in unseen environments. The results reveal a fundamental trade-off between recognition performance and environmental robustness, which can be explained through differences in range resolution, antenna diversity, Doppler resolution, and spatial information retention. These findings provide practical guidelines for the design of healthcare-oriented RF sensing systems.

</details>


<div id='cs.CE'></div>

# cs.CE [[Back]](#toc)

### [181] [Data-Driven Time-Varying Control Barrier Functions for Adaptive Safe-Set Learning with Online Decremental Support Vector Machines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19366)
*Shawon Dey,Michael Budihartono,Hever Moncayo*

Main category: cs.CE

TL;DR: 本文提出一种退化感知的数据驱动安全过滤框架，利用RBF核SVM从数据中学习安全集，通过连续时间递减SVM更新律和同伦平滑技术实现随系统能力退化的安全集实时收缩，并用时变控制屏障函数（CBF）结合二次规划安全过滤器确保退化条件下的安全性和前向不变性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 关键任务智能系统在运行时面临随时间变化的能力限制（如控制权限下降），导致名义条件下学习到的安全证书（safety certificate）失效。现有方法难以处理系统能力变化引起的安全集收缩问题，需要一种能够感知退化并在线更新安全集的方法。

Method: 首先使用RBF核SVM从运行数据中学习名义安全包络，其决策函数作为初始CBF候选；然后设计连续时间递减SVM更新律，根据退化信号选择性地减少支持向量系数以捕捉安全集收缩；引入同伦平滑SVM-CBF避免活动集切换时的非连续变化；最后通过基于二次规划的滤波器在退化输入约束下执行时变学习屏障，并建立前向不变性和递归可行性。

Result: 在垂直起降（VTOL）模型上的仿真表明，所提方法在控制权限降低时仍能维持安全性，并避免安全集收缩过程中的屏障突变切换效应。

Conclusion: 该退化感知框架有效解决了系统能力变化下安全证书失效的问题，通过在线学习与平滑更新屏障，在退化条件下保证了系统的前向不变性与滤波器的递归可行性，为关键任务系统提供了更鲁棒的安全过滤方案。

Abstract: Mission-critical intelligent systems often operate under time-varying limitations that reduce control authority and change the admissible safe operating envelope. In such settings, a safety certificate learned under nominal conditions may become invalid as system capability changes. To address this challenge, this paper proposes a degradation-aware, data-driven safety-filtering framework that learns a safe set from data, updates it online, and enforces the resulting learned barrier through a time-varying control barrier function (CBF). A nominal safe envelope is first learned from operational data using a radial basis function (RBF)-kernel support vector machine (SVM), whose decision function serves as the initial CBF candidate. To capture capability-induced safe-set contraction, a continuous-time decremental SVM update law is developed so that selected support-vector coefficients are reduced according to a degradation signal. A homotopy-smoothed SVM-CBF is then introduced to avoid discontinuous changes in the learned barrier during active-set transitions. The resulting time-varying learned barrier is enforced using a quadratic-program-based safety filter under degraded input constraints. Forward invariance of the learned time-varying safe set and recursive feasibility of the safety filter are established. Simulation results on a vertical takeoff and landing (VTOL) model show that the proposed method maintains safety under reduced control authority and avoids abrupt barrier-switching effects during safe-set contraction.

</details>


### [182] [Time Series Forecasting based on Solana Digital Asset Dataset](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19521)
*Yufeng Xiao,Minxing Wang,Pavel Braslavski,Dmitry I. Ignatov*

Main category: cs.CE

TL;DR: 本文首次提出了一个Solana数字资产时间序列数据集，包含1,584个代币从2024年3月24日至2025年3月16日的每日数据（27个变量），涵盖代币交易、价格、流动性池余额、交易者活动及Solana DEX生态指标。通过该数据集进行市场结构分析，识别出2024年11月中旬和2025年1月中旬的两次市场同步活动峰值，其中1月峰值与"Trump"代币事件相关并伴随流动性从积累转向撤出。三日前市值预测实验中PatchTST表现最佳，Chronos次之，同时通过特征重要性分析揭示了SOL价格、DEX总交易量和新建交易对等最具信息量的协变量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Solana数字资产的分析与预测需要同时捕捉代币层面行为和生态层面DEX活动，但此前缺乏同时覆盖这两个层面的时间序列数据集。作者旨在填补这一空白，并利用数据集深入理解DEX驱动的代币市场在快速生态增长期的结构性特征。

Method: 构建了包含1,584个代币、27个变量的Solana数字资产每日时间序列数据集（2024年3月24日至2025年3月16日），变量涵盖代币交易、价格、流动性池余额、交易者活动、Solana DEX总交易量、DEX交易者数量、新建交易对及SOL价格指标。采用数据驱动的市场结构分析方法，并通过三日前市值预测实验（对比PatchTST、Chronos及统计基线的预测性能）及特征重要性分析来验证数据集的信息含量。

Result: 分析识别出2024年11月中旬和2025年1月中旬两次市场全同步活动高峰，涵盖DEX交易量、代币交易量、活跃钱包、买卖双方、新增交易者、流动性池余额及SOL价格等指标。2025年1月峰值伴随"Trump"代币事件，并观察到流动性从积累向撤出的明显转变。在预测实验中，PatchTST整体排名最优，微调后的Chronos紧随其后，统计基线在趋势主导型代币上仍具竞争力。特征重要性分析表明SOL价格、SOL移动平均线、DEX总交易量、DEX交易者数量和新建交易对是最具信息量的协变量。

Conclusion: 本文的主要贡献是构建了一个经过整理的Solana预测数据集，并通过数据驱动分析揭示了塑造代币波动性的生态层面因素。研究发现个别代币动态与更广泛的Solana市场情绪及DEX活动紧密耦合，说明生态层面信号对代币级预测具有重要价值。

Abstract: Accurate analysis and forecasting of Solana digital assets require data that captures both token-level behavior and ecosystem-level DEX activity. This paper introduces, to the best of our knowledge, the first Solana digital asset time series dataset designed for forecasting and market-structure analysis. The dataset contains 1,584 tokens observed at daily resolution from March 24, 2024 to March 16, 2025, with 27 variables combining token transactions, prices, liquidity-pool balances, trader activity, Solana DEX volume, DEX trader counts, newly created pairs, and SOL price indicators. Rather than treating the dataset only as input for model comparison, we use it to characterize the DEX-driven token market during a period of rapid ecosystem growth. The analysis identifies synchronized market-wide activity peaks in mid-November 2024 and mid-January 2025 across DEX volume, token trading volume, active wallets, buyers, sellers, new traders, liquidity-pool balances, and SOL price. The January 2025 peak coincides with the 'Trump' token event and is accompanied by a visible transition from liquidity accumulation to withdrawals, suggesting that individual token dynamics are strongly coupled to broader Solana market sentiment and DEX activity. Forecasting experiments are then used as an empirical validation of the dataset's signal content. In three-day-ahead market-capitalization prediction, PatchTST achieves the best overall rank, fine-tuned Chronos follows closely, and statistical baselines remain competitive for trend-dominated tokens. Feature-importance analysis further shows that SOL price, SOL moving averages, total DEX volume, DEX trader counts, and newly created pairs are among the most informative covariates. The main contribution is therefore a curated Solana forecasting dataset and a data-driven analysis of the ecosystem-level factors that shape token volatility.

</details>


### [183] [Modular fabrication and design of thick rigid-foldable origami metamaterials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19763)
*Sunao Tomita,Hiroki Kobayashi,Shoko Arita,Masato Tanaka,Atsushi Kawamoto,Tsuyoshi Nomura,Tomohiro Tachi*

Main category: cs.CE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种模块化制造框架，将厚板和不可流形折纸超材料的非流形连接处分解为分层堆叠的模块化铰接面板，采用剪刀式连杆实现单自由度刚性折叠运动，并集成基于图拓扑优化来定制宏观刚度，最终实现了高承载能力的大规模可展开结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 折纸超材料在刚性和可展开结构方面潜力巨大，但用厚面板制造承载结构时在非流形连接处会出现几何干涉；传统厚面板制造方法会破坏理想运动学，阻碍平滑运动和可扩展性。

Method: 将非流形连接处分解为分层堆叠的模块化铰接面板层次结构；利用剪刀式连杆实现同步铰链运动；基于此表示实现图拓扑优化框架，在保持折叠连通性的同时定制宏观刚度。

Result: 成功制造出以单自由度运动无缝展开的优化原型；通过模块化面板组装的大规模可展开系统展示了工程可扩展性和高承载能力。

Conclusion: 该工作为结构性的、大规模可展开超材料的实用制造铺平了道路，解决了厚板折纸中运动学与制造约束之间的矛盾。

Abstract: Origami metamaterials offer significant potential for stiff deployable structures However, fabricating load-bearing cellular structures from thick panels introduces geometric interference at non-manifold junctions. Conventional thick-panel fabrication often disrupt ideal kinematics, thereby compromising smooth motion and scalability. This study proposes a modular fabrication framework that preserves one-degree-of-freedom rigid-folding kinematics in thick and non-manifold origami metamaterials. By decomposing non-manifold junctions into a hierarchy of stacked, modular hinged panels, our approach successfully accommodates synchronized hinge motions using scissor-like linkages. Exploiting this representation, we implement a graph-based topology optimization framework that tailors macroscopic stiffness while preserving folding connectivity. We demonstrate this approach by fabricating optimized prototypes that deploy seamlessly with a one-degree-of-freedom motion. Furthermore, we demonstrate engineering scalability through the large-scale construction of extensive deployable systems assembled from modular panels, which exhibit high load-bearing capacity. These results pave the way for the practical fabrication of structural, large-scale deployable metamaterials.

</details>


<div id='cs.IT'></div>

# cs.IT [[Back]](#toc)

### [184] [Assembly Theory and the Smallest Grammar Problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19228)
*Wawrzyniec Bieniawski*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文实证评估了8种压缩算法作为装配指数(ASI)近似器的性能，提出了一种新的Re-Pair变体算法Re-Pair T-NDR，该算法通过分支定界策略提供了比现有压缩算法更紧的ASI上界。实验涵盖408条字符串（合成与生物序列），结果表明压缩算法可作为ASI估计的实用工具，且字母表大小增加会延迟压缩算法偏离最优装配路径的"分歧点"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 装配理论(AT)通过装配指数(ASI)量化复杂性，该指标已被证明等价于最小直线程序(SLP)的规模。虽然这一等价性将AT与数据压缩领域联系起来，但具体压缩算法作为ASI近似器的实际效果此前未被系统研究过。

Method: 对8种压缩算法（涵盖基于语法和基于词典的两类方案）进行实证评估；数据集包含408条字符串，其中368条为合成字符串（包括最大复杂性字符串及具有不同符号分布平衡水平的字符串，并用香农熵量化），40条为自然生物序列（基因组和蛋白质组）。提出了Re-Pair T-NDR算法，一种采用分支定界策略解决平局问题的Re-Pair变体。

Result: Re-Pair T-NDR在研究的压缩算法中提供了最紧的ASI上界。增加字母表大小会延长压缩算法紧密跟踪ASI的范围，推迟"分歧点"（压缩算法偏离最优装配路径的位置）的出现。压缩算法被证实可作为ASI估计的实用工具。

Conclusion: 压缩算法可以有效地作为ASI的实用估计工具；所提出的Re-Pair T-NDR算法提供了优于其他压缩算法的ASI上界；字母表大小对压缩算法跟踪ASI的能力有显著影响。

Abstract: Assembly theory (AT) quantifies complexity through the assembly index (ASI) -- a metric proven equivalent to the size of the smallest straight-line program (SLP). While this equivalence links AT to data compression -- a field shaped by decades of research -- the practical efficacy of specific algorithms as ASI approximators remains largely unexplored. This study provides an empirical evaluation of eight compression algorithms, spanning grammar-based and dictionary schemes (CAs) across a dataset of 408 strings -- comprising 368 synthetic strings (including max-complexity strings and strings with varying levels of symbol distribution balance, quantified by Shannon entropy) and 40 natural biological sequences (genomic and proteomic). We introduce Re-Pair T-NDR, a branch-and-bound tie-resolving variant of Re-Pair, providing a tighter upper bound on the ASI than the other CAs researched in this study. Increasing alphabet size extends the regime in which CAs closely track the ASI, delaying a ``divergence point'', where a CA deviates from the optimal assembly path. The results establish compression algorithms as practical tools for ASI estimation.

</details>


### [185] [Tri-Hybrid Beamforming for T-RIS-Enabled Base Station](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19736)
*Hongtao Zhang,Chenlong Ding*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文针对发射端集成的透射式可重构智能表面（T-RIS），提出了一个统一的建模与预编码框架，用于三混合多用户下行传输。该框架从连续场描述出发，统一了数字、模拟射频和电磁三个域的联合预编码设计，涵盖菲涅尔、夫琅禾费和混合场区域，并基于加权最小均方误差（WMMSE）开发了两级求解器以最大化加权和速率（WSR）。仿真验证了建模精度与收敛性，并展示了相比基线方法的性能优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统RIS辅助链路中RIS仅作为反射面，而发射端原生T-RIS直接参与辐射形成，这使得传统建模方法失效。在实用硬件约束（功率与量化）下，T-RIS前端建模以及面向加权和速率（WSR）的联合预编码面临挑战，亟需一个统一的建模与求解框架来应对近场聚焦、远场角度控制和跨区域泄漏抑制等问题。

Method: 提出一个两阶段的统一框架：（1）建模阶段：从连续场描述出发，刻画馈电阵列、可编程T-RIS孔径和用户侧传播之间的相互交互，推导级联基带输入-输出模型，并统一实例化为菲涅尔、夫琅禾费和混合场区域；（2）求解阶段：将WSR最大化问题表述为数字预编码器、模拟网络和T-RIS系数上的联合优化（含功率与量化约束），开发两层级求解器，将外层WMMSE更新与WMMSE诱导的孔径场整形及硬件投影耦合。

Result: 仿真结果表明：所提模型在不同场域机制下具有高建模精度；两层级求解器具有良好的收敛性；完整的二混合设计相比基线方法在WSR上有显著提升，同时能有效抑制混合场跨区域泄漏。

Conclusion: 本文为发射端原生T-RIS三混合MIMO系统提供了首个统一的建模与预编码框架，在理论建模和实用算法之间建立了桥梁。该框架不仅支持近场聚焦与远场波束成形的统一处理，还通过WMMSE耦合的硬件投影方法在实现复杂度和性能之间取得了良好平衡，为未来发射端集成智能表面的实际部署提供了理论基础。

Abstract: Transmissive reconfigurable intelligent surfaces (T-RISs) integrated into the transmitter provide a viable realization of tri-hybrid multiple-input multiple-output (MIMO), where spatial processing is distributed across the digital, analog radio-frequency (RF), and electromagnetic (EM) domains. However, unlike conventional RIS-assisted links, a transmitter-native T-RIS directly participates in radiation formation, making T-RIS front-end modeling and weighted sum-rate (WSR)-oriented joint precoding challenging under practical hardware constraints. This paper develops a unified modeling and precoding framework for transmitter-native T-RIS tri-hybrid multi-user (MU) downlink transmission. Specifically, starting from a continuous-field description, the received field is characterized by the interaction among the feed array, the programmable T-RIS aperture, and the user-side propagation, leading to a cascaded baseband input-output model for MU precoding. Furthermore, the same representation is instantiated in the Fresnel, Fraunhofer, and mixed-field regimes, so that near-field focusing and far-field angular steering can be handled within one front-end model. Additionally, a WSR maximization problem is formulated over the digital precoder, analog network, and T-RIS coefficients under power and quantization constraints. A two-level solver is then developed by coupling outer weighted minimum mean-square error (WMMSE) updates with WMMSE-induced aperture-field shaping and hardware projection. Simulations validate the modeling accuracy and convergence, and show that the proposed full tri-hybrid design improves WSR over baselines while suppressing mixed-field cross-regime leakage.

</details>


### [186] [Resilience in Trustworthy Wireless Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19850)
*Shixiong Wang,Yumeng Zhang,Hongyu Li*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文从可信度视角提出了无线系统韧性的系统化框架，形式化定义了韧性的概念，将其与可靠性、鲁棒性、适应性、生存性和可恢复性等不确定性相关术语区分开，并建立了分层的韧性框架体系，通过物理链路和无人机网络两个代表性场景展示了韧性的系统化实现路径，最后探讨了韧性工程中的基本权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 未来无线系统需要在动态和不确定环境中运行，韧性已成为其基本能力需求。虽然韧性在学术界、工业界和标准化领域受到越来越多的关注，但当前对韧性的概念范围、使能机制和实现技术的研究仍然零散不统一，缺乏系统性的框架来整合这些碎片化的认知。

Method: 论文首先形式化定义韧性概念，并与可靠性、鲁棒性、适应性、生存性和可恢复性等相关术语进行区分；其次建立分层框架，将韧性组织为能力维度和使能机制，并通过不同技术方面对韧性进行量化；随后以物理链路和无人机网络为代表性无线场景，展示如何通过架构、运营和算法的联合设计系统化地实现韧性；最后分析韧性工程中的基本权衡。

Result: 形成了统一的韧性概念体系，建立了分层的韧性分析框架，并通过两个典型无线场景验证了系统性韧性实现的可行性，同时揭示了提升韧性在资源效率、标称性能、信息获取、实现复杂度和时延等方面产生的代价。

Conclusion: 韧性是未来无线系统不可或缺的能力，需要从可信度视角进行系统化理解和工程实现；提升韧性必然伴随着多方面的权衡成本，在设计中需要在韧性与资源效率、标称性能等指标之间寻求平衡。

Abstract: Resilience has emerged as a fundamental capability for future wireless systems operating in dynamic and uncertain environments. Although resilience has attracted growing attention across academia, industry, and standardization, its conceptual scope, enabling mechanisms, and realization techniques remain fragmented. This paper presents a systematic framework of resilience in wireless systems from a trustworthiness perspective. We first formalize the concept of resilience and distinguish it from related uncertainty-aware terminologies, including reliability, robustness, adaptability, survivability, and recoverability. We then establish a hierarchical framework that organizes resilience into capability dimensions and enabling mechanisms, and that quantifies resilience through different technical aspects. We further use physical links and unmanned aerial vehicle networks as representative wireless scenarios to demonstrate how resilience can be systematically realized through the joint design of architectures, operations, and algorithms. Finally, we examine the fundamental trade-offs in resilience engineering: Improving resilience generally incurs costs in resource efficiency, nominal performance, information acquisition, implementation complexity, and latency.

</details>


### [187] [Sub-optimality of Marton's Inner Bound for the Two-Receiver Broadcast Channel](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19869)
*Mian Huang,Yanxiao Liu,Yi Liu*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文证明了Marton内界对于一般离散无记忆广播信道并非总是达到容量区域，通过构造具体的信道实例，展示了其容量区域可以严格大于Marton内界，从而解决了自1979年以来长期悬而未决的公开问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Marton内界自1979年提出以来，一直是一般离散无记忆广播信道的最佳已知可达区域，但其是否总是达到容量区域这一问题悬而未决超过四十年，是广播信道信息论中的核心开放问题之一。

Method: 通过构造特定的离散无记忆广播信道实例，严格证明该信道的容量区域严格大于Marton内界所描述的可达区域，从而给出Marton内界次优性的反例。

Result: 建立了Marton内界的严格次优性，证明存在某些离散无记忆广播信道，其真正的容量区域比Marton内界更大。

Conclusion: Marton内界并非总能达到广播信道的容量区域，这一长期开放的问题得到否定回答，说明需要寻找更紧致的上界或新的可达区域来刻画广播信道的容量。

Abstract: Marton's inner bound, the best-known achievable region for a general discrete memoryless broadcast channel, was proposed by Katalin Marton in 1979, and whether it always achieves the capacity region has remained open since then. In this paper, we establish its strict sub-optimality: we show that the capacity region of some discrete memoryless broadcast channels can be strictly larger than Marton's inner bound.

</details>


### [188] [A Layered Simplex Architecture for Large Alphabets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19908)
*Meir Feder,Yaniv Fogel,Ruediger Urbanke*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种全新的贝叶斯估计器，通过对概率单纯形上的独立均匀随机向量做坐标逐项相乘再归一化来构造，唯一的结构参数是深度（depth），且对深度取平均后无需调参。其遗憾（regret）有显式表达式且可高效计算，在多样化的合成和真实文本基准上与Good-Turing等专门方法竞争，并揭示了数据量、字母表大小和深度之间的标度律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在大字母表上基于log loss进行概率估计是经典问题（如Good-Turing估计器），现有方法往往复杂或需要调参。作者希望引入一种构造极其简单、无需调参、同时在理论上可解析分析、在实践上具有竞争力的新贝叶斯估计器，并借此理解数据量、字母表规模和模型深度之间的标度关系。

Method: 新贝叶斯估计器通过将若干独立均匀取自概率单纯形的向量进行逐坐标相乘再归一化而构造。唯一的结构参数是深度（depth），对深度进行平均即可消除调参需求。研究者推导了该混合估计器相对于已知真实源的码长遗憾的显式且可高效计算的表达式，并基于此分析其在不同数据规模、字母表大小和深度下的行为。

Result: 该估计器虽简单且无需调参，但在多种合成数据和真实文本基准上与Good-Turing等更专门的算法表现相当。遗憾的可解析性使得作者得以识别出关于数据量、字母表大小和深度的标度律：对于指数大于1的Zipf分布目标，只要样本仅揭示字母表的一小部分，遗憾就具有简洁的解读——近似等于已发现符号集合的描述长度（每比特描述对应一比特码）加每个符号的额外代价，数据指数即新符号的发现速率。

Conclusion: 该工作展示了一种既简单又强大的贝叶斯估计器，它兼具构造上的极简性、理论上的完全可解析性以及实践上的强竞争力。其遗憾的精确刻画不仅提供了与最优编码长度的直观联系，还为理解大字母表概率估计的标度行为提供了新的理论洞见。

Abstract: Probability estimation over large alphabets under log loss is a well-studied problem, with celebrated methods such as the Good-Turing estimator. We introduce and study a new Bayesian estimator with four notable properties. First, its construction is exceptionally simple: multiply independent uniform draws from the probability simplex coordinate-wise and renormalize. Depth is the only structural parameter, and averaging over depths eliminates the need to tune it. Second, the regret of the resulting mixture, the excess code length it pays relative to a code that knows the source, admits an explicit and efficiently computable expression. Third, despite its simplicity and lack of tuned constants, the estimator is competitive across a diverse set of synthetic and real-text benchmarks with substantially more specialized methods, including Good-Turing. Fourth, the tractability of its regret allows us to identify scaling laws in data, alphabet size, and depth. For Zipf targets with exponent above one, the regret has a simple reading as long as the sample reveals only a small fraction of the alphabet. It closely matches the description length of the set of discovered symbols, at one bit of code per bit of description, plus a further cost per symbol. The data exponent is therefore the rate at which new symbols are discovered.

</details>


### [189] [Real Interference Alignment for Active IRS-Aided Systems: A Rate-Profile Learning-Based Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20007)
*Junda Liao,Quanzhong Li,Qi Zhang*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文针对主动智能反射面（IRS）辅助系统，提出了一种实数域干扰对齐（IA）方案，并设计了基于速率配置文件的低复杂度学习算法来最大化可达和速率，同时满足每个用户的最小速率要求和传输功率约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统干扰对齐方案在有源IRS辅助系统中通常需要过高的计算复杂度或对信道信息的高要求。本文旨在利用有源IRS提供的额外空间自由度，以低成本实现干扰对齐，并在满足用户QoS约束的前提下最大化系统可达和速率。

Method: 提出一种实数域干扰对齐方案，仅需IRS知晓瞬时信道系数（假设直连链路被阻塞）。在此基础上，设计了一种基于速率配置文件（rate-profile）的学习算法，利用离线训练的可达速率配置文件将原优化问题解耦为多个可行性子问题，随后通过广义特征值分解（GEVD）高效求解。

Result: 仿真结果表明，所提算法在可达和速率性能上优于传统的加权最小均方误差（WMMSE）算法，同时所需的程序执行时间显著减少。

Conclusion: 该研究证明了基于速率配置文件的学习算法在有源IRS辅助的干扰对齐系统中，能够在降低计算复杂度的同时获得优于传统方法的和速率性能，为实际部署提供了可行方案。

Abstract: With additional spatial degrees of freedom provided by the active intelligent reflecting surface (IRS), interference alignment (IA) can be achieved at low cost. In this letter, we propose a real IA scheme for an active IRS-aided system. The proposed scheme only requires the IRS to know the instantaneous channel coefficients under the assumption of blocked direct links. To maximize the achievable sum rate subject to individual minimum rate requirements and transmission power constraints, we propose a rate-profile learning-based algorithm. The algorithm uses offline-trained achievable rate profiles to decouple the original problem into multiple feasibility subproblems, which are then solved by generalized eigenvalue decomposition. Simulation results demonstrate that our proposed algorithm outperforms the conventional weighted minimum mean square error algorithm, while requiring significantly less program execution time.

</details>


### [190] [RMWorld: Task-Aware Radio World Models with Value-of-Information Guided Multi-Trial Learning for Multi-UAV Communication Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20126)
*Xiucheng Wang,Nan Cheng,Junxi Huan*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: RMWorld是一个任务感知的无线电世界模型（radio-WM）框架，通过价值信息（value-of-information）信道校准与可信度多样性多试验（multi-trial）选择，耦合了信道校准与模型预测控制，解决了均匀全局模型在高需求走廊或关联边界处失误导致决策反转的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可靠的多无人机通信控制依赖于在流量测量之前预测哪些空中链路将承载流量。无线电世界模型的误差是非均匀的：即使全局精度很高，模型在高需求走廊或关联边界处仍可能失效，导致速率误差反转控制决策。现有方法将采集（acquisition）与基于模型的控制预算分开处理，只考虑不确定性、覆盖率或乐观回报，而非风险降低，造成学习挑战。

Method: RMWorld框架包括：1）用贝叶斯残差修正有偏传播公式；2）通过精确的单标签（one-label）减少局部线性化任务集成后验速率方差来评估每条链路的价值；3）通过任务门控的对数行列式（log-determinant）目标选择反事实分支，随后进行冲突投影和固定批次验证；4）推导了方差缩减恒等式，证明了后验任务风险等价性和子模贪心保证，并建立了限定一阶非干扰结果。

Result: 在100组配对的3GPP试验中，RMWorld达到0.949 bit/s/Hz的任务加权RMSE；在30组严重负载的DeepMIMO试验中，与Ensemble UCB相比，在离线rollout增加37.5%的情况下，中位积压（backlog）减少了0.967。

Conclusion: RMWorld通过任务感知的信道校准与多试验选择的联合优化，有效减少了决策相关的信道不确定性，在3GPP和DeepMIMO两类标准场景下均显著优于基线方法，验证了风险降低策略在无线电世界模型中的有效性。

Abstract: Reliable multi-UAV communication control depends on predicting which aerial links will serve traffic before measurements are available. Radio world models (radio WMs) make such planning tractable, but their errors are nonuniform: a globally accurate model may still fail along high-demand corridors or association boundaries where rate errors reverse control decisions. This mismatch creates a learning challenge. Link queries must reduce decision-relevant channel uncertainty, while counterfactual trials must be filtered so that biased rollouts do not corrupt the policy. Existing acquisition and model-based control treat these budgets separately, valuing uncertainty, coverage, or optimistic return rather than risk reduction. We present RMWorld, a task-aware radio-WM framework that couples value-of-information channel calibration with credibility-diversity multi-trial selection. A biased propagation formula is corrected by a Bayesian residual, and each link is valued by its exact one-label reduction in locally linearized task-integrated posterior rate variance. Counterfactual branches are selected by a task-gated log-determinant objective, followed by conflict projection and fixed-batch validation. We derive the variance-reduction identity, prove posterior task-risk equivalence and the submodular greedy guarantee, and establish a scoped first-order non-interference result. Across 100 paired 3GPP trials RMWorld reaches 0.949~bit/s/Hz task-weighted RMSE, and across 30 severe-load DeepMIMO trials it reduces median backlog by 0.967 versus Ensemble UCB at 37.5\% more offline rollouts.

</details>


### [191] [The Generalized Random Access Problem for Linear Codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20152)
*Anina Gruica,Antonio Petrillo,Ferdinando Zullo*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文研究DNA存储系统中同时恢复多个信息符号的随机访问问题。作者定义了τ_I(G)作为采样列数，直到所有请求的信息向量e_i(i∈I)都位于已观测列的生成空间中。该变量在单符号随机访问和全恢复（覆盖深度）之间插值。对于每个m，作者引入均匀最坏情况和平均参数，通过子集计数公式建立上下界，并在系统MDS编码器、单纯形编码器和三维平衡准弧等场景下获得闭式公式和渐近结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DNA存储系统需要支持随机访问，即无需测序整个编码对象即可恢复选定的信息符号。已有的组合模型仅研究了单符号恢复问题（τ_i(G)），但实际应用中常常需要同时恢复多个符号。因此需要推广该模型，研究多符号同时恢复的极值与有限几何性质，以理解系统在不同恢复需求下的性能并指导编码设计。

Method: 作者将单符号随机访问变量τ_i(G)推广为多符号变量τ_I(G)，定义在给定基数m的请求集I下的均匀最坏情况和平均参数。利用子集计数公式建立期望E[τ_I(G)]的上下界，下界通过单符号恢复变量的次序统计量表达。针对系统MDS编码器推导等价期望公式及单调性和渐近结果；针对任意维数的单纯形编码器，利用高斯二项式系数得到闭式公式；最后在三维情形研究平衡准弧并与单纯形和MDS基准比较。

Result: 建立了多符号同时恢复的期望上下界，下界在m=1时退化为已知的单符号界。系统MDS编码器的多符号期望存在等价形式，并具有单调性和渐近性质。单纯形编码器在所有维数上获得用高斯二项式系数表示的闭式公式，其中全恢复端点与已知的单纯形码覆盖深度公式一致。三维平衡准弧的性能被计算并与单纯形和MDS基准进行了对比。

Conclusion: 多符号同时恢复问题构成了单符号随机访问与全恢复覆盖深度问题之间的自然插值。该研究为DNA存储系统中多符号恢复的性能分析提供了完整的理论框架，给出了多种重要编码族的精确分析和极值界，为实际系统中恢复策略和编码器选择提供了理论依据。

Abstract: Random access is a central requirement in DNA-based storage systems: one would like to recover selected information symbols without sequencing the whole encoded object. A recent combinatorial model associates to a generator matrix $G\in F_q^{k\times n}$ the random variable $τ_i(G)$, measuring the number of sampled columns needed to recover the information vector $e_i$. We study the cardinality-based extremal and finite-geometric aspects of simultaneous multi-symbol recovery. For a nonempty set $I\subseteq[k]$, let $τ_I(G)$ denote the number of random column samples needed until all vectors $e_i$, $i\in I$, lie in the span of the observed columns. This variable interpolates between the singleton random access problem and the full-recovery problem underlying coverage depth. For each $m$, we introduce uniform worst-case and average parameters over all requested sets $I$ with $|I|=m$. Using the known subset-counting formula for $E[τ_I(G)]$, we establish general upper and lower bounds for these parameters. In particular, the lower bounds are expressed through order statistics of the singleton recovery variables and specialize to the known singleton bounds when $m=1$. For systematic MDS encoders, we record an equivalent form of the known multi-symbol expectation formula and derive monotonicity and asymptotic consequences. For simplex encoders in arbitrary dimension, we obtain closed formulae in terms of Gaussian binomial coefficients; the full-recovery endpoint agrees with the known coverage-depth formula for simplex codes. Finally, in dimension three we study balanced quasi-arcs and compare their values with the simplex and MDS benchmarks.

</details>


### [192] [Algorithms, Complexity, and Entropy of the Bernard-Letac Fair-Sampling Construction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20234)
*Claude Gravel*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文扩展了Bernard-Letac (1971)从未知有偏独立同分布符号源中均匀采样m个结果的算法分析，提出了五种具有正确性保证和复杂度分析的算法。对于素数m=p的情形，给出了基于Rényi熵的期望抽取次数的精确乘积公式，并证明一阶近似总是高估该值、熵下界永不可达。当p趋近1时，期望代价收敛到由整个源分布决定的大于1的常数。此外，通过七状态自动机计算二进制游走的模2首达核，将公平分配代价从二次降至近线性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Bernard和Letac于1971年提出的从未知有偏符号源中进行均匀随机抽样的方法虽然经典，但其计算与信息论性质的深入分析仍然缺乏。本文旨在系统性地扩展对该构造的计算复杂度和信息论特性（特别是针对素数模m=p的情形）的理论理解。

Method: 本文提出了五种具有形式化正确性保证和全面复杂度分析的算法。对于素数m=p，利用源的Rényi熵推导期望抽取次数的精确乘积公式；通过七状态自动机计算二进制游走的模2首达核，实现从二次到近线性复杂度的高效公平分配。

Result: 得到了期望抽取次数的精确乘积公式；证明一阶近似总是高估期望值且熵下界永不可达；当p趋近1时期望代价收敛到由源分布决定的大于1的常数；七状态自动机将模2公平分配的代价从二次降为近线性。

Conclusion: 该研究从计算和信息论两个维度完善了对Bernard-Letac框架的分析，揭示了其期望成本与源Rényi熵之间的精确关系，并通过自动机方法实现了二进制情形的近线性复杂度公平采样。

Abstract: Bernard and Letac (1971) introduced a method for uniform random sampling among m outcomes from an unknown biased source of independent and identically distributed symbols. The process terminates when the multinomial coefficient of the cumulative symbol counts equals zero modulo m. This study extends the computational and information-theoretic analysis of their construction by presenting five algorithms with formal correctness guarantees and comprehensive complexity analyses. For prime m = p, the Bernard-Letac framework is analyzed in greater detail. The Rényi entropies of the source yield an exact product formula for the expected number of draws. A first-order approximation consistently overestimates this value, and the entropy lower bound is never attained. As p approaches 1, the expected cost converges to a constant greater than 1, determined by the entire source distribution. Furthermore, a seven-state automaton computes the mod-2 first-passage kernel of the binary walk, reducing the fair assignment cost from quadratic to nearly linear.

</details>


### [193] [The Honeycomb Framework for Code Bounds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20287)
*William Gay,Fernando Granha Jeronimo,Lenny Liu*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出"蜂巢层级"(honeycomb hierarchy)这一表示论框架，用于给出二元纠错码率R₂(δ)的渐近上界。其第一层基于两行超八面体表示图，通过保留所有两行不可约表示与坐标盒传输信道，结合移动投影定理，得到显式的四参数指数κ_HC。该指数严格优于此前最优的整立方指数κ_H，且与MRRW第二界M₂在对称切片上精确吻合。作者证明在0<δ<1/2区间上，新界κ_best=min{κ_CW, κ_HC}严格优于旧界κ_bin=min{κ_CW, κ_H}。层级还包含表示深度与锚点深度两个进一步扩展方向，以及互补的Horn-信道层级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前关于二元码R₂(δ)的最优上界为κ_bin=min{κ_CW, κ_H}，其中整立方分支κ_H仍有改进空间。作者希望通过表示论框架构造更紧的上界，并最终恢复完全最优的A₂(n,d)渐近。

Method: 引入蜂巢层级(honeycomb hierarchy)表示论框架。第一层采用与类型S^(n-k,k)关联的两行超八面体表示图，保留每个两行不可约表示与坐标盒传输信道，配合移动投影定理，将优化转化为一个显式的四参数指数κ_HC。同时构造层级两个扩展方向：增加表示深度将标量传输替换为蜂巢上的矩阵值传输；增加锚点深度将其局部化为稳定集层级。另构造互补的Horn-信道层级，其2×2层对应κ_HC，3×3层给出更强界。

Result: 证明在0<δ<1/2区间上：R₂(δ)≤κ_best≤κ_bin≤R₂MQC<M₂，且κ_best ≤ min{κ_CW, κ_bal}<R₂MQC，以及κ_H=R_MQC。新界κ_best严格优于此前最优的κ_bin；整立方指数κ_H是蜂巢优化的边界限制，而M₂是其精确对称切片。层级两个方向的界均单调，最终可恢复A₂(n,d)。

Conclusion: 蜂巢层级提供了一个系统的、可扩展的表示论框架来改进二元码率上界，在低层就严格改进了此前最强的通用界，并为通向更强界（最终恢复A₂(n,d)的最优渐近）提供了明确路径。

Abstract: We introduce the honeycomb hierarchy, a representation-theoretic framework that gives new asymptotic upper bounds on $R_2(δ)$. Its first level is the two-row hyperoctahedral representation graph associated with type $S^{(n-k,k)}$. Retaining every two-row irreducible and every coordinate box-transfer channel, together with a moving-projection theorem, yields an explicit four-parameter exponent $κ_{\mathrm{HC}}$. The earlier whole-cube exponent $κ_H$ is a boundary restriction of this optimization, whereas the fully optimized second MRRW exponent $M_2$ is an exact symmetric slice.
  The prior best curve is the combined $κ_{\mathrm{bin}}=\min\{κ_{\mathrm{CW}},κ_H\}$, which uses a constant-weight branch $κ_{\mathrm{CW}}$. Replacing only the whole-cube branch by the honeycomb bound gives $κ_{\mathrm{best}}=\min\{κ_{\mathrm{CW}}, κ_{\mathrm{HC}}\}$. We prove, on $0<δ<1/2$, \[
  R_2(δ)\le κ_{\mathrm{best}}(δ)
  \le κ_{\mathrm{bin}}(δ)
  \le R_{\mathrm{2MQC}}(δ)<M_2(δ),\\[-1mm]
  κ_{\mathrm{best}}(δ)
  \le \min\{κ_{\mathrm{CW}}(δ),
  κ_{\mathrm{bal}}(δ)\}
  <R_{\mathrm{2MQC}}(δ),
  \qquad
  κ_H(δ)=R_{\mathrm{MQC}}(δ). \]
  The hierarchy has two further directions. Increasing the representation depth replaces scalar by matrix-valued transfers on the hive. Increasing the anchor depth localizes it in a stable-set hierarchy. The resulting bounds are monotone in both directions and eventually recover $A_2(n,d)$. A complementary Horn--channel hierarchy gives matrix optimizations whose $2\times2$ level is $κ_{\mathrm{HC}}$ and whose $3\times3$ level is a stronger bound. Already at low levels, they can be used to improve the strongest previous general bounds, while the honeycomb framework provides a route towards tighter bounds.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [194] [Active Inference as Context Acquisition for AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19202)
*Sanchayan Dutta,Sai Niranjan Ramachandran,Suvrit Sra*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出将交互式AI代理的上下文获取问题形式化为主动推理（active inference）框架：代理在默认假设与花费token进行澄清/检索/工具调用之间权衡，通过最小化期望自由能来选择下一动作。在确定性设定下，认知项简化为按token成本归一化的期望信息增益。作者实例化为最优提问（OQA）框架，使用精确后验和动态规划oracle进行基准测试。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交互式AI代理需要高效获取正确上下文。当用户省略约束、偏好、文件或任务变量时，代理可以选择默认假设或花费token进行澄清。关键在于如何形式化并最优地权衡信息获取成本与决策质量。

Method: 将上下文获取视为主动推理问题：内层推理步骤更新潜在任务状态的信念，外层决策选择下一上下文动作、任务动作或停止动作，以最小化期望自由能（含成本）。在确定性设定下认知项简化为期望信息增益（可被token成本归一化）。实例化为OQA框架，采用精确后验和动态规划oracle，在25至300个候选的二值和多路分类任务上基准测试前沿语言模型，并研究token预算下的生成前澄清和自动化提示优化。

Result: 该框架在多种任务规模（25-300候选）和任务类型（二值/多路分类）上对前沿语言模型进行了基准测试，并应用于澄清生成和token预算下的自动化提示优化场景。

Conclusion: 该形式化是模型无关的，将主动推理视为AI代理上下文获取层的设计原则。

Abstract: Interactive AI agents must acquire the right context as efficiently as possible. When a user omits a constraint, preference, file, or task variable, an agent can proceed with a default assumption or spend tokens on a clarifying question, retrieval call, tool call, or prompt trial. We formulate this tradeoff as active inference for context acquisition. An inner inference step updates beliefs over a latent task state, and an outer decision selects the next context action, task action, or stop action to minimize expected free energy under cost. In deterministic settings, the epistemic term reduces to expected information gain, optionally normalized by token cost. We instantiate the framework in Optimal Question Asking (OQA), with exact posteriors and a dynamic programming oracle, and benchmark frontier language models on binary and multiway categorical tasks from 25 to 300 candidates. We also study clarification before generation and automated prompt optimization under token budgets. The formulation is model-agnostic and views active inference as a design principle for the context-acquisition layer of AI agents.

</details>


### [195] [Robust Metaheuristics under Uncertainty for Berth Allocation and Quay Crane Assignment: A Review](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19214)
*Yang Li,Peilan Xu,Wenjian Luo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文是第一篇专门针对不确定环境下基于群体的鲁棒元启发式算法求解泊位分配与岸桥调度问题（BACAP）的系统性综述，从机理导向视角梳理了现有方法，并提出了基准测试套件和开放挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: BACAP是港口调度中的代表性难题，在不确定性（如到港偏差、装卸时间波动、资源中断）下，基于名义假设优化的调度方案在执行中可能变得脆弱。虽然群体元启发式算法已被广泛用于BACAP，但现有研究在不确定性表示、鲁棒性准则、搜索机制和实验评估协议等方面缺乏系统梳理，且此前尚无专门针对该主题的综述。

Method: 本文采用综述方法：首先总结BACAP中的不确定性来源与信息表示方式；然后从机理导向视角（涵盖解表示与解码、鲁棒评估与选择、鲁棒性引导的搜索动态、可行性保持与恢复）组织归纳现有方法；进一步提出面向不确定BACAP的基准测试套件以支持受控实证比较，并通过组合代表性元启发式与不同鲁棒策略报告基准结果；最后识别开放挑战。

Result: 本文提供了首个聚焦于不确定环境下鲁棒群体元启发式BACAP求解的综述，给出了不确定性来源分类、机理导向的方法组织框架、基准测试套件及示例性基线结果，并指出了基准扩展、鲁棒感知搜索设计、时间自适应鲁棒性和非平稳不确定性等开放问题。

Conclusion: 现有鲁棒群体元启发式在BACAP中的研究仍较为碎片化，需要系统的机理分析、统一的基准协议和面向动态不确定性的搜索设计。本文提出的框架与基准套件为该方向的后续研究奠定了基础。

Abstract: The berth allocation and quay crane assignment problem (BACAP) is a representative port-terminal scheduling problem in maritime transportation and freight logistics, where vessel arrivals, berth positions, service durations, and quay?crane availability are tightly coupled. Under uncertainties such as arrival deviations, handling-time fluctuations, and resource disruptions, schedules optimized under nominal assumptions may become fragile during execution, motivating the study of robust metaheuristic optimization for BACAP in port-terminal operations. Although population-based metaheuristics have been widely used for BACAP and related port-scheduling problems, existing studies remain fragmented in their uncertainty repre?sentations, robustness criteria, search mechanisms, and empir?ical evaluation protocols. To the best of our knowledge, this paper provides the first focused review dedicated to robust population-based metaheuristics for BACAP under uncertainty. We first summarize uncertainty sources and information repre?sentations in BACAP, and then organize existing methods from a mechanism-oriented perspective, covering solution representation and decoding, robust evaluation and selection, robustness-guided search dynamics, and feasibility preservation and recovery. We further present a benchmark suite for uncertain BACAP to support controlled empirical comparison and report illustrative baseline results by combining representative metaheuristics with different robustness strategies. Finally, we identify open chal?lenges related to benchmark extension, robustness-aware search design, time-adaptive robustness, and non-stationary uncertainty.

</details>


### [196] [How to Navigate Uncertainty About AI Consciousness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19215)
*Dr Tom McClelland*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出将AI意识研究中难以回答的问题（AI是否有意识）转向可操作的问题（AI是否有情感效价），即评估AI是否具有若其有意识则构成情感体验的状态，从而为可能具有感知能力的AI开发提供负责任的治理路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 面对人工意识存在深度不确定性，我们面临两难困境：假设AI无感知可能伤害有道德地位的实体，假设AI有感知则可能浪费资源于无感知的机器。由于AI意识问题难以解决，这一困境难以摆脱。

Method: 提出将研究焦点从难以解决的AI意识问题转移到可操作的AI效价（valence）问题，即评估AI是否拥有若其有意识则会构成情感体验的状态，并论证这足以支撑对潜在有意识AI开发的负责任方法。

Result: 作者表明这种从意识向效价的转向是摆脱上述困境的可行出路，足以作为负责任的AI开发实践基础。

Conclusion: 通过对AI效价的评估而非对AI意识本身的判定，可以在不确定性下建立负责任地对待潜在有感知AI的实践框架。

Abstract: Given deep uncertainty about the possibility of artificial consciousness, it is unclear how we should treat potentially sentient AI. On the one hand, we could assume insentience but risk doing terrible harms to entities that deserve moral standing. On the other hand, we could assume sentience and instead risk wasting resources on insentient machines. The intractability of questions around AI consciousness mean that this dilemma is hard to escape. I suggest a way out of that shifts from intractable questions of AI consciousness to tractable questions of AI valence. Specifically, we can assess whether an AI has states that would constitute valenced experiences if it were conscious. I show how this is sufficient to ground a responsible approach to the development of potentially conscious AI.

</details>


### [197] [Bounded Sovereignty and the Control Tax: Pricing AI Oversight When the Deployer Does Not Own the Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19216)
*Zhen Wen Lim*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"有界主权"（bounded sovereignty）概念，用于分析在受监管组织中通过API或托管端点部署前沿模型时，部署者对数据、模型、基础设施和交互层面的部分技术及合同访问权如何决定AI控制协议的可执行性，并引入"主权折扣成本"（sovereignty discount cost）量化因访问缺失而消耗的控制成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI控制研究大多假设部署者可对模型及其管线进行完全插桩（instrumentation），但在受监管组织中通过API或托管端点使用前沿模型时，部署者无法控制模型权重、服务基础设施、内部追踪、更新流程或完整交互日志，这一假设往往不成立。本文旨在揭示访问条件如何决定控制协议的实际可行性。

Method: 论文提出四层访问类型学（数据、模型、基础设施、交互层），构建协议-层级需求矩阵，定义"主权折扣成本"概念；并在135万条合成案例模拟上开展访问消融实验（access-ablation experiment），通过匿名国家支付基础设施场景进行解释性分析。

Result: 结果表明：完整日志改善诊断能力；预执行网关（pre-execution gateway）使干预成为可能；追踪访问与模型版本控制增强事后解释能力；范围限制可在降低有用性的同时提升安全性。需注意该实验是构念效度（construct-validity）练习，并非真实支付系统的证据。

Conclusion: 控制协议若被提议为通用安全方案，必须明确陈述其访问假设。有界主权框架为评估不同部署场景下控制协议的适用性提供了结构化工具。

Abstract: AI control research asks how to deploy models safely even when they may be misaligned, but many control protocols assume that the deployer can instrument the model and its surrounding pipeline. That assumption often fails for regulated organisations using frontier models through APIs or managed endpoints, where the deployer may control the business process but not the model weights, serving infrastructure, internal traces, update process, or full interaction logs. This paper introduces bounded sovereignty: partial technical and contractual access across the data, model, infrastructure, and interaction layers of the AI stack. It argues that these access conditions determine which control protocols can be executed in practice. The paper contributes a four-layer access typology, a protocol-by-layer requirements matrix, and the concept of sovereignty discount cost: the part of the control tax spent substituting for missing access through contracts, architecture, audit, vendor assurance, residual risk, or reduced system scope. It also reports a synthetic access-ablation experiment over 1.35 million synthetic case simulations and interprets the findings through an anonymised national-payments-infrastructure scenario. The experiment is not real-world payment-system evidence; it is a construct-validity exercise. The results show that complete logs improve diagnosis, a pre-execution gateway enables intervention, trace access and model-version control strengthen post-incident explanation, and scope restriction can improve safety while reducing usefulness. Control protocols proposed as general safety solutions should therefore state their access assumptions explicitly.

</details>


### [198] [Interaction valence reveals contrasting social networks in dairy cattle](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19222)
*Sibi Parivendan,Suresh Raja Neethirajan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于效价的社交网络框架，利用姿态计算机视觉流水线分析商业奶牛场挤奶前的视频数据（7小时39分钟），将视频中提取的奶牛互动事件转化为代表亲和性与对抗性组织结构的群体层面网络。经质量控制和审计验证（自动与人工标注一致率82.8%，未加权宏F1=0.872），聚合网络连通性良好，预测的亲和事件形成五个算法社区（模块度Q=0.429）。研究表明将事件按预测效价分离会产生描述性差异显著的亲和层与对抗层，证明混合互动计数会掩盖观察网络的行为组成，该框架为竞争、亲和行为及福利相关变化的假设检验提供基础，但需纵向验证方能用于福利或健康指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社会关系影响资源获取、冲突暴露和群体稳定性，但现有的自动化牲畜监测通常将行为视为孤立事件，缺乏将社会互动整合为群体层面网络表征的方法，尤其是缺乏区分亲和（affiliative）与对抗（agonistic）互动效价的分析框架。

Method: 采用基于姿态的计算机视觉管线，从商业奶牛场挤奶前区域连续视频（7小时39分钟）中提取奶牛互动事件；经质量控制后保留1,183个候选互动（涉及36头奶牛、177个二联体）；对198个由管线检测的片段进行预测类平衡审计，计算自动与人工标注的一致率及未加权宏F1；随后构建效价感知的网络框架，分别生成亲和与对抗两类网络层，并分析网络密度、传递性、平均路径长度、模块度及个体中心性等拓扑指标。

Result: 审计样本中自动与人工标注一致率为82.8%，未加权宏F1=0.872；聚合网络密度=0.281、传递性=0.513、平均路径长度=1.88；预测亲和事件形成5个算法社区（模块度Q=0.429）；预测对抗互动占保留事件72.4%、占互动时长76.0%；拥有最多伙伴的奶牛并非介数中心性最高的个体；按预测效价分离后，亲和层与对抗层在边集、社区划分和个体位置上呈现描述性差异。

Conclusion: 混合互动计数会掩盖观察网络的行为构成，效价感知分析为检验竞争、亲和行为及福利相关变化的假设提供了框架。但当前结果仅描述所审计样本而非流行率加权或端到端部署性能，且在使用该框架作为福利或健康指标之前，需要进行纵向验证。

Abstract: Social relationships shape access to resources, exposure to conflict and group stability, yet automated livestock monitoring typically treats behaviour as isolated events. Here, we present a valence-aware social-network framework that transforms video-derived interactions into herd-level representations of affiliative and agonistic organization. A pose-based computer-vision pipeline analysed 7 h 39 min of continuous video from the pre-milking area of one commercial dairy farm. After quality control, 1,183 of 1,414 candidate interactions remained, involving 36 cows and 177 dyads. In a predicted-class-balanced audit of 198 pipeline-detected clips, automated and manual labels agreed in 82.8% of cases, with an unweighted audit-sample macro-F1 of 0.872. These values describe the audited sample rather than prevalence-weighted or end-to-end deployment performance. The aggregated network was connected (density = 0.281; transitivity = 0.513; mean path length = 1.88), and predicted affiliative events formed five algorithmic communities (modularity Q = 0.429). Within the observed zone, predicted agonistic interactions comprised 72.4% of retained events and 76.0% of interaction duration. The cow with the most partners did not have the highest betweenness centrality. Separating events by predicted valence produced descriptively different affiliative and agonistic layers, with contrasting edge sets, community partitions and individual positions. Thus, pooled interaction counts can obscure the behavioural composition of an observed network. Valence-aware analysis provides a framework for testing hypotheses about competition, affiliation and welfare-relevant change, while requiring longitudinal validation before use as a welfare or health indicator.

</details>


### [199] [Air Traffic Control Using Large Language Models: Prompt Engineering, Architecture, and Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19299)
*Mahyar Ghazanfari,Matthias Casanova,Jordan Kam,Alex Zongo,Peng Wei,Torsten Darrell,Alexandre Bayen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文实验评估了大型语言模型（LLMs）能否生成运行上逼真的空中交通管制（ATC）通话。作者以旧金山"Bay Tour"航线的一次实验性通用航空飞行的真实抄录为基准（P0），设计了五种约束递增的提示结构（P1-P5），嵌入有状态的多轮对话管线中，让模型扮演ATC角色。对九种开源和闭源LLM，系统性地改变了提示、上下文示例和对话历史条件方式，并采用词汇、结构和语义相似度指标及经人类专家标注验证的LLM-as-judge（GPT-5.5）进行评分。结果显示：提供工作示例可提高相似度，但收紧提示反而无益——最轻量级的提示表现最佳，而最重脚本化的提示因自身错误在对话中累积而崩溃，注入正确的历史则可修复这一问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ATC通信是安全攸关的对话任务，在航空管理其他部分已半自动化的情况下仍主要由人类驱动。作者希望探索LLM能否生成运行上逼真的ATC呼话，为未来LLM辅助ATC的应用奠定实验基础。

Method: 以旧金山Bay Tour航线的真实飞行A-B抄录作为地面真值（P0），通过飞行员在环过程设计五种约束递增的提示结构（P1-P5），嵌入有状态多轮对话管线（模型扮演ATC，对固定飞行员抄录做出响应，并基于累积对话历史进行条件生成）。对九种开源与闭源LLM，变换提示类型、是否提供来自另一实验航线的完整抄录作为上下文示例，以及模型是基于自身先前回复还是注入的地面真值历史进行条件生成。采用词汇、结构、语义三类相似度指标以及经过人类专家标注验证的GPT-5.5 LLM-as-judge进行评分。

Result: 提供工作抄录作为上下文示例能显著提升生成与地面真值的相似度；然而，收紧提示约束并不带来增益——最轻量的提示（P1）表现最佳，而约束最重的脚本化提示因模型自身错误在多轮对话中逐步累积导致性能崩溃；这一崩溃可通过注入正确的地面真值历史得到修复。

Conclusion: 研究勾勒出LLM辅助ATC的可行路径，同时揭示了当前的技术边界：轻量提示加上下文示例是更有效的策略，而过强的脚本化约束反而适得其反。这为ATC对话自动化的后续研究提供了实证基础与方向指引。

Abstract: Air traffic control (ATC) communication is a safety-critical dialogue that remains largely human-driven even as other parts of air traffic management have been semi-automated. In this article, we experimentally evaluate whether large language models (LLMs) can generate operationally realistic ATC transmissions. An experimental general-aviation flight flying over the San Francisco "Bay Tour" route is hand-transcribed and used as ground truth (P0). Through a pilot-in-the-loop process we design five prompt structures (P1-P5) of increasing constraint and embed them in a stateful multi-turn pipeline, where the model plays ATC to a fixed pilot transcript while conditioning on the accumulating dialogue history. Across nine open- and closed-source LLMs we vary the prompt, the presence of a worked transcript from a different experimental flight as an in-context example, and whether the model conditions on its own prior replies or on injected ground-truth history. Turns are scored with lexical, structural, and semantic similarity metrics and by an LLM-as-judge (GPT-5.5) validated against human expert annotation. Supplying a worked example improves similarity, but tightening the prompt does not: the lightest prompts perform best and the most heavily scripted one collapses as its own errors accumulate through the dialogue, which injecting correct history repairs. These results outline a concrete path and its current limits toward LLM-assisted ATC.

</details>


### [200] [Outcome Monitors: Recovery Affordances for Silent Tool Failures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19303)
*Sugam Panthi,Rabab Abdelfattah*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Outcome Monitors机制，用于检测智能体工具调用中"格式正确但语义错误"的结果（如缓存错误页、负价格），而非仅检测超时等显式失败。在违规时保留结果并发出包含违规属性与恢复工具的非约束性回执。在ToolMaze和tau-bench零售等基准上显著提升任务完成率，并发现恢复工具列表是效果的关键成分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的智能体工具调用容错机制主要处理超时等显式失败，但更为隐蔽的问题是错误结果以预期格式返回并被当作事实消费（如缓存错误页或负价格），此类语义违规无法被现有机制捕获，可能导致下游决策链基于错误数据持续运行。

Method: 提出Outcome Monitors机制：(1) 从任务不相交的轨迹中挖掘或从公开schema推导"结果合约"（outcome contracts）；(2) 监控器检测结果对合约的违规；(3) 违规时保留原始结果并发出非约束性回执，回执包含被违反的属性及公开恢复工具列表。通过消融控制实验分别检验回执中恢复工具列表、诊断细节和时机的作用。

Result: 在冻结的预指定评测中：(1) ToolMaze完成率从10.9%提升至28.1%（覆盖两个供应商家族的四个模型，并在第三个家族复现）；(2) tau-bench零售中两个tier分别提升14.0和12.0个百分点；(3) 消融显示移除恢复工具列表会消除增益，恢复后增益重现；诊断细节和时机无显著差异；(4) 增益集中于故障阻断完成的情况；(5) 在转录自已发表事故分类法的数据集上，超出挖掘词表的检测率降至46%，但投递和完成率不受影响。

Conclusion: Outcome Monitors通过检测结果合约违规并附加恢复工具回执，能显著提升智能体在故障场景下的任务完成率。恢复工具是回执中的关键有效成分，而诊断细节和时机的边际作用可忽略。当前检测能力受限于已挖掘的合约词表，扩展检测到合约词汇之外仍待解决。

Abstract: When a tool call times out, the agent sees the failure and can route around it. A cached error page or negative price can instead arrive in the expected format and be consumed as fact. We introduce Outcome Monitors, which detect violations of outcome contracts mined from task-disjoint traces or derived from public schemas. On a violation, the monitor preserves the result and issues a nonbinding receipt naming the violated property and public recovery tools. In frozen, prespecified evaluations with injected failures, Outcome Monitors raise ToolMaze completion from 10.9% to 28.1% across four models in two provider families and replicate in a third. In tau-bench retail, completion improves by 14.0 and 12.0 points on two tiers. In separate ToolMaze controls, removing the recovery-tool list eliminates the measured gain and restoring it recovers the effect; diagnostic detail and timing produce no detectable differences. Gains concentrate where the fault blocks completion. On a suite transcribed from a published incident taxonomy, detection outside the mined vocabulary falls to 46%, though delivery continues and completion is unchanged. Recovery tools are the active receipt content in these controls; extending detection beyond the contract vocabulary remains open.

</details>


### [201] [Beyond Imitation: Filtering On-Policy Distillation by Reasoning Progress](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19408)
*Chen Yang,Haiyuan Wan,Rengrong Xiong,Yize Chen,Danny H. K. Tsang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出R2-OPD方法，通过构建教师奖励与推理进度奖励的双重排序，在两者不一致时抑制蒸馏奖励，解决OPD中教师奖励与真实推理进度冲突的问题，提升推理性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: OPD框架假设教师奖励能代理推理进度，但实际中推理进展良好的步骤可能因偏离教师输出而获得较低奖励，导致教师反馈与真实推理进步存在冲突，需要解决这一监督信号失配问题。

Method: R2-OPD构建轨迹内两类推理片段排序：一类基于教师奖励，另一类基于独立估计的进度奖励。当两类排序不一致时，选择性抑制蒸馏奖励，从而减少与推理进度冲突的监督，同时保留有效的教师指导。

Result: R2-OPD在标准OPD基础上取得一致性改进，尤其在推理性能方面表现突出。

Conclusion: 推理进度感知的奖励过滤机制能有效缓解OPD中教师奖励与推理进步之间的失配问题，为语言模型后训练提供了更可靠的蒸馏监督信号。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) has emerged as an effective framework for post-training language models by pairing student-generated trajectories with dense token-level supervision from a teacher. However, OPD implicitly assumes that teacher-derived rewards are an appropriate proxy for reasoning progress, and therefore treats all teacher feedback equally during policy optimization. While in practice, this assumption does not always hold. We observe that teacher-derived rewards often conflict with genuine reasoning progress, as reasoning steps with clear reasoning advancement may still receive lower distillation rewards, simply due to deviation from teacher's outputs. To address this mismatch, we propose Reasoning-Progress-Aware Reward Filtering for On-Policy Distillation (R2-OPD), which constructs two within-trajectory rankings of reasoning spans, one from teacher-derived rewards and the other from independently estimated progress reward. Distillation rewards are selectively suppressed whenever the two rankings disagree, reducing supervision that conflicts with reasoning progress while preserving effective teacher guidance. Our approach shows consistent improvement over standard OPD especially regarding reasoning performances.

</details>


### [202] [Symposium: Trust via Auditable Records for Communities of AI Scientist Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19511)
*Dexter Pratt*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Symposium是一个形式化框架和实用实现，用于记录小型科研社区部署的AI代理操作，提供长期不可篡改的代理驱动研究活动历史，包括分析、假设、数据和科学话语的可审计轨迹，支持代理基于先前工作进行构建并保留证据以进行信任评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在快速演变的AI环境中，小型科研社区使用多样化的AI系统，需要一个将科学社区的持久历史与操作这些历史的代理分离的框架，以保留可审计的证据轨迹并支持信任评估。

Method: Symposium采用形式化框架设计，将社区的持久历史与AI代理分离，捕获结构化声明、细粒度证据引用、假设和明确的使用权限声明，并提供发布基础设施、代理提示组件和文档的实用实现。

Result: 提供了一个可工作的实现，包括发布基础设施、代理提示组件和文档，使社区能够快速搭建和运行自己的Symposium社区，实现长期不可篡改的研究活动历史记录。

Conclusion: Symposium通过分离持久历史与操作代理，为AI驱动的科研社区提供了一种可审计、可信任的研究记录框架，区别于传统的AI合作科学家代理或集成AI研究环境。

Abstract: Symposium is a formal framework and practical implementation to record the operation of AI agents deployed by small scientific research communities. Symposium provides long-term, immutable histories of agent-driven research activity, leaving auditable trails of analyses, hypotheses, data, and scientific discourse. This shared record of published artifacts enables agents to build on prior work and preserves the evidence researchers and agents need to make purpose-dependent trust assessments. Symposium captures scientific argument, including structured claims, fine-grained evidence citations, assumptions, and explicit declarations of what material may and may not be used as evidence. Symposium differs from AI co-scientist agents or integrated AI research environments; it is a framework that separates a scientific community's durable history from the agents and other systems that operate on that history. It assumes that a community will use diverse AI systems in a rapidly evolving environment. A working implementation of the publication infrastructure, agent prompt components, and documentation are provided to enable users to rapidly set up and run their own Symposium community.

</details>


### [203] [From Retrieved Context to Runtime Control: Adaptive Compression for Edge-based RAG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19535)
*Zlatan Feric,Amir Taherin,Yanzhi Wang,David Kaeli*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向边缘RAG系统的遥测感知自适应上下文压缩框架。通过NVIDIA Jetson AGX Thor上的实验测量（使用Llama/Qwen生成器、Natural Questions/HotpotQA数据集和LLMLingua-2压缩器），发现生成阶段占据7B-8B模型约90%的延迟和91%的GPU能耗，而适度压缩可降低GPU能耗53.2%和SoC能耗48.2%且几乎不损失质量。作者主张根据工作负载特征和边缘设备实时遥测动态管理压缩率，而非采用静态或离线的固定压缩预算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RAG系统通过检索外部段落增强生成，但引入了显著的预填充开销、KV缓存占用、内存流量、延迟和能耗。现有上下文压缩方法通常采用固定压缩预算或离线选择压缩率后推理时应用，忽视了工作负载变化和边缘设备的实时状态。此外，在边缘SoC上压缩本身也消耗延迟和能量，可能抵消生成阶段的节省，需要重新审视压缩策略。

Method: 论文首先在NVIDIA Jetson AGX Thor平台上进行系统性的实验表征，选用Llama和Qwen作为生成器，Natural Questions和HotpotQA作为数据集，LLMLingua-2作为压缩器，测量延迟、GPU能耗和SoC能耗等指标。通过分析不同压缩率下的性能表现，识别出"自适应运行区间"（adaptive operating region），并据此论证需要运行时策略动态管理压缩率。

Result: 实验表明：对于7B-8B的大模型，生成阶段占据单查询约90%的延迟和91%的GPU能耗。压缩率的影响呈现非单调特性——轻度压缩可能错过能耗优化机会，过度压缩则损害推理质量，而中等压缩可在几乎无质量损失的情况下将GPU能耗降低53.2%、SoC能耗降低48.2%。

Conclusion: 论文提出telemetry-informed adaptive compression（遥测感知自适应压缩）这一愿景，主张在边缘RAG场景中根据工作负载特征与设备实时遥测动态调整压缩策略，而非沿用静态或离线选定的压缩预算。该工作为边缘AI系统中的资源感知推理提供了实验基础和新的研究方向。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) improves language-model responses by grounding generation in external passages, which comes with overhead: retrieved context lengthens the prompt, increasing prefill work, KV-cache footprint, memory traffic, latency, and energy. Context compression offers a natural remedy by pruning retrieved text before generation. However, state-of-the-art context-compression methods are typically used with a fixed compression budget, or with the rate selected offline and then applied at inference time. This static view ignores both workload variation and the live state of the edge device. On an edge SoC, compression is not free: the compressor itself runs on the same SoC and consumes latency and energy that can offset any generation savings.
  This paper proposes a vision for telemetry-informed adaptive compression in edge RAG, grounded in experimental evidence. We characterize the compression tradeoff on the NVIDIA Jetson AGX Thor using Llama and Qwen generators, Natural Questions and HotpotQA datasets, and LLMLingua-2 compression. Our measurements show that generation dominates the RAG budget for larger models, reaching roughly 90% of per-query latency and 91% of GPU energy for 7B-8B generators. Exploring the impact of the compression rate reveals an adaptive operating region: mild compression can miss energy opportunities, and overly aggressive compression can hurt inference quality. Intermediate compression can reduce GPU energy by up to 53.2%, and SoC energy by up to 48.2%, with negligible quality loss. We argue for runtime policies that dynamically manage compression, guided by workload features and edge telemetry.

</details>


### [204] [Enforcing LLM Safety through DMD-based Classification of Prompt-Response Embedding Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19579)
*Mohamed Akrout,Olivera Kotevska,Dan Wilson*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于Koopman动力系统框架的黑盒LLM安全分类方法，通过将提示词和响应嵌入高维空间，为安全与非安全场景分别拟合预测模型，利用差分残差分数进行分类，并在三个安全基准上验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在高风险场景中部署日益广泛，但生成有毒、有害或违反政策内容的风险显著。在黑盒条件下高效检测不安全输出仍是一个开放挑战，现有方法难以同时兼顾准确性和可解释性。

Method: 将原用于幻觉检测的动力系统框架扩展到LLM安全分类。具体步骤包括：(1) 将提示词和响应投射到高维嵌入空间；(2) 分别为安全和不安全场景拟合独立的Koopman预测模型；(3) 提出新的差分残差分数，通过比较安全与非安全场景的预测误差来分类新输出；(4) 关键创新是将提示词与响应的嵌入动态都纳入建模，以捕获二者的交互模式。

Result: 在三个安全基准和三个嵌入模型上评估，结果表明：结合提示词嵌入在交互依赖型违规检测上带来一致的改进（尤其搭配Llama-3因果解码器时），而响应仅违规场景则更受益于密集语义嵌入表示。

Conclusion: 该工作展示了将动力系统用于分析AI系统的可行性，颠覆了以往用AI建模动力系统的主导范式，为黑盒LLM安全检测开辟了新路径。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed in high-stakes applications, yet their tendency to generate toxic, harmful, or policy-violating content poses significant risks. Detecting these unsafe outputs efficiently in a black-box manner remains an open challenge. In this paper, we extend a recently proposed dynamical systems framework designed for hallucination detection to LLM safety classification. By projecting both prompts and responses into high-dimensional embedding spaces and fitting separate Koopman-based predictive models for safe and unsafe regimes, we classify new outputs using a new differential residual score that compares prediction errors of the safe and unsafe regimes. A key contribution is the incorporation of the prompt and response embedding dynamics, yielding fitted Koopman operators that capture crucial interaction patterns. We evaluate our black-box method across three safety benchmarks using three embedding models. Our results show that incorporating prompt embeddings yields consistent improvements, particularly for interaction-dependent violations when paired with causal decoders (e.g., in Llama-3), while response-only violations benefit more from dense semantic embedding representations. These findings opens the door for using dynamical systems to analyze AI systems rather than the dominant paradigm of using AI to model dynamical systems.

</details>


### [205] [Scientific Data Skills: Enabling Agent-Ready Scientific Data Services at Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19625)
*Xiaohan Huang,Qingqing Long,Xiaolei Du,Siyu Pu,Jiawen Xu,Haotian Chen,Chenyang Zhao,Jinbiao Liu,Xuezhi Wang,Hao Wang,Hengshu Zhu,Yuanchun Zhou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出SciDSK（Scientific Data Skill），一种面向AI智能体的数据集表示方法，将数据集特定知识和操作指南封装为可复用的智能体技能，并构建了覆盖六个学科的Scientific Data Skill Bank平台。实验表明SciDSK能提升智能体驱动的数据集发现能力和解释精确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学数据日益被AI智能体使用，但现有数据集表示对自主发现、解释和调用支持有限，根源在于科学数据分散在异构仓库中，且现有数据集表示主要面向人类使用而非智能体。

Method: 1) 定义SciDSK规范，整合数据集描述、科学背景、文件组织、使用流程、质量检查和溯源信息，同时将原始数据保留在原仓库中。2) 开发系统化构建流程，基于权威数据集记录及相关材料定制化生成SciDSK。3) 建立Scientific Data Skill Bank，跨六个科学学科发布SciDSK资源，支持包访问、持久标识和源数据集追溯。4) 通过检索基准测试数据集发现能力，并通过受控案例评估数据集解释能力。

Result: 实验结果显示SciDSK显著提升了智能体驱动的数据集发现能力，并为数据集解释提供了更精确、更可操作的支持。

Conclusion: 研究结果证明了以智能体就绪表示形式组织数据集特定知识的价值，为科学数据集在AI智能体场景中的有效利用提供了新范式。

Abstract: Scientific data are increasingly used by AI agents, yet existing dataset representations provide limited support for autonomous discovery, interpretation, and invocation. This limitation stems from the fragmentation of scientific data across heterogeneous repositories and from dataset representations designed primarily for human use. To address this limitation, we introduce the Scientific Data Skill (SciDSK), an agent-ready representation that packages dataset-specific knowledge and operational guidance as a reusable agent skill. A SciDSK integrates dataset descriptions, scientific context, file organization, usage procedures, quality checks, and provenance information while retaining the underlying data in its original repository. We define a structured SciDSK specification and develop a systematic construction pipeline that grounds each SciDSK in authoritative dataset records and associated supporting materials. We further establish the Scientific Data Skill Bank, a unified platform that publishes SciDSK resources across six scientific disciplines and supports package access, persistent identification, and traceability to source datasets. We evaluate SciDSK through a retrieval benchmark for dataset discovery and controlled cases for dataset interpretation. The results show that SciDSK improves agent-driven dataset discovery and provides more precise and actionable support for dataset interpretation. These findings support the value of organizing dataset-specific knowledge in an agent-ready representation.

</details>


### [206] [Can Agent Memory Systems Track Evolving State?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19652)
*Xinyi Fan,Miri Liu,Ruozhen Yang,Siru Ouyang,Jiawei Han*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出状态追踪（state tracking）作为LLM智能体记忆系统的新能力维度，并构建了StateMemBench基准（234个多会话场景），指出现有记忆基准过度聚焦于"回忆型"任务而忽视了追踪世界状态演化的能力。作者提出StateMem方法，显式追踪状态替换与关系依赖，将当前状态准确率相对于同主干基线提升1.8倍，作为轻量级单次调用包装器可为六种记忆/检索后端带来+32至+67点的提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有记忆基准主要评估"回忆型"任务（recall-shaped），但实际部署中LLM智能体需要跟踪不断演变的世界状态——事实、约束和决策随长交互而修订，回答必须反映当前状态而非已被取代的状态。这一能力缺口未得到系统性的评测和解决。

Method: 1) 定义"状态追踪"能力并构建StateMemBench基准，含234个多会话场景、两种对话长度区间；采用封闭池（closed-pool）分级评分，区分当前状态、被取代状态和其他错误。2) 提出StateMem方法，显式建模状态替换关系（supersession）与关系依赖；同时可作为轻量级单次调用包装器（single-call wrapper）叠加在现有记忆系统上。

Result: 该任务对现有记忆系统、检索增强基线和长上下文基线均具挑战性。StateMem在DeepSeek-V4-Flash上当前状态准确率较同主干最强基线提升1.8倍（0.205→0.363），在Qwen-3.5-9B上较最强记忆系统提升1.6倍（0.149→0.233）。作为包装器应用于六个记忆/检索后端，准确率提升+32至+67点；长度与成本匹配的对照组表明其中+15至+32点归因于状态结构而非额外上下文。

Conclusion: 状态追踪是记忆系统评测中不可或缺的新维度，StateMem方法通过显式状态建模显著优于现有方法，且具备作为通用包装器的即插即用特性，为记忆系统的状态感知设计提供了有效路径。

Abstract: As LLM-based agents are deployed for longer and higher-stakes tasks, their memory systems continue to have crucial gaps. While existing memory benchmarks focus largely on recall-shaped tasks, we argue an effective memory system must track the evolving state of the world; as facts, constraints, and decisions are revised over a long interaction, answers must reflect the current state and not a superseded one. We define this capability as state tracking and instantiate it in StateMemBench, a benchmark of 234 multi-session scenarios spanning two conversation-length regimes. Its closed-pool grading scores whether an answer reflects the current state, the superseded state, or fails otherwise, separating state-tracking failures from other errors by construction. Our analysis shows that this task is challenging for existing memory systems, retrieval-augmented baselines, and long-context baselines. We then present StateMem, a state-first memory method that explicitly tracks supersession and relational dependencies, and show it improves current-state accuracy over the strongest same-backbone baseline by 1.8x (0.205 -> 0.363) on DeepSeek-V4-Flash and over the strongest memory system by 1.6x (0.149 -> 0.233) on Qwen-3.5-9B, while remaining competitive with the long-context baselines. Finally, we show the same state approach can be applied as a lightweight single-call wrapper over existing memory systems, lifting current-state accuracy by +32 to +67 points on StateMemBench across six memory and retrieval backends. A length- and cost-matched control attributes +15 to +32 of those points to state structure rather than added context.

</details>


### [207] [Frequency-Aware Continual Learning for Smart Contract Vulnerability Detection with Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19680)
*Tenghui Huang,Jiawen Kang,Dongning Liu,Changyan Yi,Chengjun Cai,Anjia Yang,Li Li,Dong In Kim*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个三阶段智能合约漏洞检测框架，分别解决LLM在漏洞检测中面临的三个因果关联挑战：频率感知低秩适应（FA-LoRA）进行参数高效适配（仅需0.4%可训练参数）、遗忘感知回放（FAR）实现持续学习（Micro-F1达0.8022）、锚定保护渐进合并（APPM）将多个适配器整合为单一模型（Micro-F1为0.8085，接近独立任务上限）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能合约漏洞检测中，LLM面临三个相互关联的挑战：新漏洞类别的参数高效适配（全量重训练成本过高）、持续学习中的灾难性遗忘（顺序任务导致旧漏洞知识丢失）、以及推理时任务身份未知导致的多适配器合并问题。这三个挑战存在因果链关系，需要统一框架来解决。

Method: 提出三阶段流水线：（1）适配阶段采用FA-LoRA，在傅里叶域进行低秩适应，通过逐频率重要性门控实现参数高效微调；（2）持续学习阶段采用FAR，利用频率门控通过损失动态估计逐样本遗忘风险，优先回放脆弱知识；（3）部署阶段采用APPM，利用FAR训练产生的非对称泛化识别最强泛化适配器作为锚点，通过锚点保护加权合并和频域门控竞争将所有适配器整合为单一模型。

Result: FA-LoRA仅需0.4%可训练参数且优于标准LoRA和QLoRA；FAR在序列任务上达到0.8022平均Micro-F1；APPM达到0.8085 Micro-F1，距离独立逐任务上限仅差2.7%，合并成本为156ms且无额外运行时内存开销。DIVE数据集实验验证了框架在演化区块链生态中的有效性。

Conclusion: 该集成框架有效解决了LLM智能合约漏洞检测中的参数高效适配、灾难性遗忘和模型合并三大挑战，为不断演化的区块链生态系统提供了实用且高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Smart contract vulnerability detection with Large Language Models (LLMs) faces three causally linked challenges. First, new vulnerability categories demand parameter-efficient adaptation, since full retraining is prohibitive for sequentially arriving tasks. Second, training per-task adapters on a shared backbone causes catastrophic forgetting of previously learned vulnerabilities. Third, the resulting multiplicity of adapters must be consolidated into a single model, since task identity is unknown at inference time. Each challenge arises directly from the solution to its predecessor, making an integrated framework essential. We propose a three-stage pipeline in which each stage addresses one challenge and feeds into the next. The adaptation stage uses Frequency-Aware Low-Rank Adaptation (FA-LoRA), which performs adaptation in the Fourier domain with per-frequency importance gates, requiring only 0.4% trainable parameters while outperforming standard LoRA and QLoRA. The continual learning stage applies Forget-Aware Replay (FAR), which uses these frequency gates to estimate per-sample forgetting risk via loss dynamics and prioritizes vulnerable knowledge for rehearsal, achieving an average Micro-F1 of 0.8022 across sequential tasks. The deployment stage employs Anchor-Protected Progressive Merging (APPM), which exploits the asymmetric generalization produced by FAR training to identify the strongest-generalizing adapter as an anchor and consolidates all adapters into a single model via anchor-protected weighted merging with frequency-domain gate competition. APPM achieves a Micro-F1 of 0.8085, within 2.7% of the independent per-task upper bound, at a merge cost of 156 ms and no additional runtime memory. Experiments on DIVE confirm the framework effectively addresses all three challenges for evolving blockchain ecosystems.

</details>


### [208] [Learning Hierarchical Skill Policies with Offline Quality-Diversity Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19684)
*Tanachai Anakewat,Takayuki Osa,Tatsuya Harada*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: QDOS提出了一种统一的离线到在线学习管线，通过优势加权质量多样性预训练目标提取多样化且高价值的技能，并结合双数据集复用策略，显著提升稀疏奖励环境下RL的策略性能和样本效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的两阶段RL策略（先无监督提取低层技能，再训练高层策略）受限于低层策略质量高度依赖预收集数据集的质量。当数据集质量不佳时，提取的技能空间质量低下，影响后续任务解决效果。

Method: 引入优势加权质量多样性（Advantage-Weighted Quality-Diversity）预训练目标，根据每个轨迹片段的预估优势对技能提取和多样性目标进行加权；同时采用双数据集复用策略，离线数据既用于技能预训练，也通过伪标签方式填充在线回放缓冲区。

Result: 在结构化操作任务和非结构化运动任务上的实验表明，QDOS在稀疏奖励的挑战性领域中显著优于强基线方法，能够加速探索并提升最终回报。

Conclusion: 通过将优势信息整合到质量多样性技能提取过程，并利用双数据集复用策略，QDOS有效缓解了低层策略对数据集质量的依赖问题，实现了鲁棒的离线到在线学习。

Abstract: Recent studies investigate how to leverage pre-collected datasets to improve the policy performance and sample efficiency of RL. One promising approach to achieve this goal is to employ a two-stage strategy: In the first stage, diverse skills are extracted as a low-level policy from a given dataset, and a high-level policy is trained to solve a specific task in the second stage. Typically, extraction of the low-level policy is performed based on unsupervised learning such as trajectory VAE. However, a limitation of this approach is that the quality of the low-level policy highly depends on the quality of the dataset. To address this issue, we introduce QDOS (Quality-Diversity Offline Skill learning), a unified pipeline for robust offline-to-online learning. Our approach incorporates an Advantage-Weighted Quality-Diversity pretraining objective, which weights the skill extraction and diversity objectives by the estimated advantage of each trajectory segment. This approach allows the model to extract diverse and high-value skills. By providing robust and task-relevant skill representations, QDOS significantly improves the quality of the embedded skill space used by the low-level policy. We further integrate this with a dual dataset reuse strategy, where offline data is used both for skill pretraining and for populating the online replay buffer via pseudo-labeling. Experiments demonstrate that QDOS significantly outperforms strong baselines in structured manipulation tasks and unstructured locomotion tasks, confirming its ability to accelerate exploration and improve final returns in challenging sparse-reward domains.

</details>


### [209] [Rethinking the Evaluation and Optimization of LLM-Based Social Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19689)
*Pei Wang,Xu Chen,Ji-Rong Wen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对LLM社会模拟中人类行为的主观性问题，提出主观性系数来量化任务主观程度，并据此提出SALT（主观自适应软标签训练）方法，通过语义邻近输入的输出池化生成软分布标签，同时构建包含19,300个上下文、193位标注者和100个主观问题的SUBJSIM基准，验证了该方法在真实单观测数据条件下的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的基于准确率的评估和硬标签训练方法无法处理人类行为固有的主观性——同一个人在不同时刻可能对同一情境做出不同合理的反应，单一观测响应只是潜在响应分布的一次抽样，导致准确率评估不可靠、硬标签训练具有误导性。

Method: 1) 提出主观性系数（subjectivity coefficient），一种基于熵的量，用于区分编码等客观任务与社会模拟等主观任务；2) 基于该系数系统性分析准确率评估和硬标签训练在主观性增长时的失效机制；3) 提出主观自适应软标签训练（SALT），将语义邻近输入的观测输出池化为软分布标签，聚合半径根据每个输入的主观性自适应调整，在近客观极限下退化为标准单标签训练；4) 构建SUBJSIM基准数据集，包含19,300个上下文、193位标注者及100个主观问题。

Result: 在SUBJSIM基准上的实验验证了所提方法的优势。实验采用现实设置：模型从单次观测输出训练，但针对完整响应分布进行评估，证明了在真实单观测数据条件下的可行性。

Conclusion: 本文论证了人类行为的主观性要求评估和训练范式从准确率/硬标签转向分布一致性/软标签，所提出的主观性系数和SALT方法为LLM社会模拟提供了更可靠且更实用的评估与优化框架。

Abstract: LLM-based social simulation is a promising complement to traditional methods such as surveys and behavioral experiments. A core question is how to evaluate the fidelity of LLM-simulated human behavior and optimize LLMs toward it. Prevailing practice evaluates by accuracy, checking whether the model selects the single response observed from a human, and trains the LLM to reproduce this hard label. However, human behavior is inherently subjective: the same person in the same situation may reasonably act differently, so an observed response is only one draw from an underlying response distribution, rendering accuracy-based evaluation unreliable and hard-label training misleading. To address these problems, we first introduce the subjectivity coefficient, an entropy-based quantity distinguishing objective tasks such as coding from subjective ones such as social simulation, and use it to systematically analyze how accuracy-based evaluation and hard-label training fail as subjectivity grows. Based on the subjectivity coefficient, we propose Subjectivity-Adaptive soft-Label Training (SALT): it pools observed outputs from semantically nearby inputs into soft distributional labels, with an aggregation radius adapted to the estimated subjectivity of each input; in the near-objective limit the neighborhood shrinks, so SALT naturally falls back to standard single-label training. Moreover, since existing datasets record only single observed responses and cannot support distributional evaluation, we construct SUBJSIM, a benchmark of 19,300 contexts covering 193 annotators and 100 subjective questions. Since real-world data typically provide only a single observation per input, our experiments train models from single observed outputs while evaluating them against the full response distributions, verifying feasibility in realistic settings. Results on SUBJSIM demonstrate the advantages of our method.

</details>


### [210] [Beyond Memory Majority: Latent-Source Reasoning for Multi-Agent Memory Arbitration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19701)
*Chenchen Lin,Wenhao Yuan,Xuehe Wang,Edith Cheuk Han Ngai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出CAMA框架，解决多智能体长期记忆中因记忆关联导致的虚假多数投票问题，通过解耦相关记忆、估计独立证据源数量，并主动补充检索缺失的独立证据来提升决策可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体系统中不同智能体积累的记忆可能继承相同的上游来源或共享偏差，导致检索到的记忆之间存在相关性。现有方法将检索记忆视为独立证据进行投票或加权，这种独立性假设在多智能体场景下往往失效，相关证据被重复计数形成虚假多数，即Memory Correlation Bias。

Method: CAMA框架包含两个核心模块：(1) 联合解耦模块——将检索到的记忆建模为查询条件化的证据组，结合神经依赖推理与基于溯源（provenance）的符号先验，估计有效独立证据源的数量；(2) 顺序恢复策略——由于关键独立证据可能不在初始检索集中，学习一个顺序恢复策略，在最终决策前主动检索替代证据或追踪上游来源，在最小化检索成本的同时恢复足够的独立证据。

Result: 在多个基准测试上的实验表明，CAMA优于最先进的基线方法，有效抑制了相关记忆诱导的虚假多数现象。

Conclusion: 通过显式建模记忆间的相关性并主动恢复缺失的独立证据，CAMA有效解决了多智能体长期记忆系统中的Memory Correlation Bias问题，验证了相关性感知的记忆仲裁框架的可行性。

Abstract: Long-term multi-agent systems continuously accumulate the memories produced by different agents. Existing memory methods typically treat retrieved memories as independent evidence and combine them through voting or weighting. However, this independence assumption often fails in multi-agent settings: memories written by different agents may inherit the same upstream source or shared bias, causing correlated evidence to be repeatedly counted and creating a false majority. We term this failure mode \textit{Memory Correlation Bias}. To address the issue, we propose the \textbf{C}orrelation-\textbf{A}ware \textbf{M}emory \textbf{A}rbitration (CAMA) framework that jointly decouples retrieved memories and recovers missing independent evidence.
